# Changelog

All notable changes to RPG Reactor will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Changed

- **An icon can be put into a name without knowing the syntax or counting cells, and [SV] Attack Motions reads its weapon types.** Two follow-ups to drawing `\I[n]` codes as icons. The Attack Motions tab shows each weapon type as table text rather than in a dropdown, so the `<select>` shim never reached it and a type named `\I[80]Sword` still read as markup in the row and again in the title of the dialog that row opens; both now render through the same renderer. The larger half is the other direction — *authoring* a code. Names on the Types tab and every field on the Terms tab are edited as plain text, which is correct (they are where a code is written, and hiding it would leave no way to type one) but meant an author had to know the `\I[n]` syntax and the cell number by heart, with IconSet.png open in an image viewer to count. Their shared right-click menu gains **Insert Icon...**, which opens the same 2x IconSet grid the Items and Skills pages use to pick an `iconIndex` and writes the code instead of a field. Because that menu is shared, one entry covers the Types name editor and all of Terms — basic, commands, params and messages alike. The Types tab's own row menu gets it too, where there is no caret to insert at: from there the code goes to the front of the name and *replaces* one already there rather than stacking a second in front of it, so the command reads as "set this type's icon". The grid opens on the icon the name already carries, the code lands on the caret and replaces the selection, and a field that was removed or rewritten while the modal was open is left alone rather than receiving someone else's icon. `Insert Icon...` is translated in all 17 non-English locales.

- **Element and skill-type names show their icon instead of their icon code.** These names carry their icon inside the name - `\I[78]Special`, `\I[64]Fire` - because the System type lists are plain string arrays with nowhere else to put one, and `drawTextEx` expands the code wherever a window draws the name that way. (Whether it does is per-window rather than per-name: the stock `Window_Command` and `Window_EquipSlot` paths use `drawText` and print the code, while the plugins that expect the convention replace them with `drawTextEx` versions that sniff for `\I[n]`. Either way it is the name the author wrote, and the editor should show what it means.) The editor showed the stored string verbatim, so the Traits list of an actor, class, weapon, armor, enemy, state or item read `Add Skill Type / \I[78]Special`, and every dropdown naming an element or a skill type showed markup where the icon belongs: Edit Trait's Element Rate, Attack Element and Add/Seal Skill Type, the Skill and Item pages' Skill Type and damage Element, and System 2's [SV] Magic Skills rows. A new `RRIconCodes` splits a stored name into the icons and text it stands for and draws each icon as a 20px crop of `img/system/IconSet.png` - the same cell size and the same URL builder the database list's own icons use, so one sheet reads at one size whichever panel it appears in. Two surfaces call it. The `<select>` shim is the larger: it already replaces every native dropdown popup with a div-based one, which is the only reason this is possible at all - a native `<option>` can hold nothing but text - so painting its trigger label and its option rows through the renderer gave every dropdown in the editor icons at once rather than a list of the ones someone remembered. Its type-to-filter now matches the name as shown, so a code's digits no longer compete with the name being typed. The seven trait tables render their value cell through it too, as do the equip-type slot labels on the Actors page's Equipment section and in the battle-test dialog - the same names the trait rows were already drawing, two panels away. Editing surfaces deliberately keep the raw string: the Types tab and the Terms tab are where a code is authored, and hiding it there would leave no way to type one - nothing here touches stored data. With no project open, or no IconSet the host can resolve, the code is dropped and the name reads clean rather than leaving an empty box. The sheet's URL is cached against its own mtime, so replacing IconSet.png shows up without restarting the editor while an encrypted project does not re-inline the whole sheet per icon. Covered by `icon-codes.test.cjs`, which runs the shim over a real `<select>` of element names and inspects the cells it built, alongside the split, escaping and fallback rules.

## [0.98.4] - 2026-08-31

### Fixed
- Project switch: opening a project over another no longer shows the first map black (3D view now disabled and re-framed across the switch; cross-project model preview cache cleared).
- Browser games launch scaled to their window (runtime `20260830.34`): stretch mode now defaults on everywhere, not just desktop/mobile builds; F3 still toggles 1:1.
- Web playtest console cleanup (runtime `20260830.33`): video-surface AbortErrors at scene switches silenced (lifecycle, not failure), onError no longer prints `undefined undefined`, and the 3D map effect preview warms the effects folder on web instead of erroring.
- Enemy AI (GitHub #33): Target State conditions work on plugin string scopes (`<Target: ...>`) — candidates group via probe Game_Action predicates; action ratings are 1-9 across all locales.
- Backdrop-close sweep: 110 `target === modal/overlay` close handlers excised across 102 files (brace-aware, listener shells kept); only UIManager's stateless confirm/alert/reload dialogs keep the gesture; guarded by `no-backdrop-close.test.cjs`.
- The event page's inline model preview centres skinned characters properly: their measured box can collapse at the feet (bind-pose vertices the skeleton hides), and subtracting that centre aimed the camera at the ankles — the declared extent places the middle when the box is clearly degenerate.
- `scanMessageLine` consumes the punctuation codes (. | ! > < ^ $ after a backslash) as zero-width; in-game parity proof via `fx-msgparity.cjs` (TextCodes injected into the live game: identical width and identical cut index against `Bitmap.measureTextWidth`).
- The message editor re-reads System.json on every open, so changing Main Font (or font size) in System 2 re-tunes the overflow measurement immediately — no editor restart.
- Message width model corrected against live game truth (`fx-msgwidth.cjs`: boxWidth 1272/padding 12/innerWidth 1248 on the 1280 Demo): `BOX_MARGIN 4`, `WINDOW_PADDING 12`, `NEW_LINE_X 4`, `FACE_MARGIN 164` replace the 4+8 model; `scanMessageLine` quotes single-token font families so `context.font` cannot reject the project font.
- `MessageBoxes.collectRun` walks backward to the run's first box and returns `startIndex`/`activeIndex`; `collectOne` means the box containing the index; the event list and troop editor splice at `run.startIndex` and the message dialog opens on `activeIndex`.
- The Show Text guide is now a per-line overflow marker instead of a single ruler column: each line is measured the way the game draws it — escape codes consumed (colours cost nothing, icons cost 36px, font-size steps move the pen, actor/party/currency codes measure as what they will actually say) — and only the characters that genuinely won't fit are highlighted, with the pixel excess in the tooltip. A ruler column could never be right: codes cost columns in the editor but nothing in game, and a proportional font gives every line a different budget.
- The Show Text guide line now measures against the project's own resolution (UI area width) and font size instead of MZ's stock 816px box — a 1280-wide game's dialogue no longer trips the guide with plenty of room left in the window. A plugin-configured message width still wins.
- The 3D Models editor's orbit centre gets the same guard: measured bind-pose bounds win over an under-declared extent.
- Event model preview: characters whose skinned exports declare a too-small extent centred on their feet — the measured bind-pose bounds now win when taller; and rotating counts as input, so the preview holds full frame rate through a ring drag instead of throttling to the idle rate.
- The Change Maximum dialog (all database sections and Types) keeps its rounded bottom corners (the footer carried square ones past the boundary) and no longer closes on a click beside it — Cancel and the cross close it, nothing else.
- Parked background scripts (runtime `20260830.26`): `Game_Map.updateInterpreter` moves an interpreter holding in a `BACKGROUND_WAIT_MODES` wait onto `$gameMap._reactorParked` (serialized with the map) when another event starts, gives the map a fresh `Game_Interpreter`, ticks parked runners each frame and unlocks their events on completion; the self-retrigger guard covers parked event ids.
- Preview-model clicks: `eventAt` box-tests `modelPreview` roots alongside the pickables; `highlight` returns early for them (a Group has no material and its scale is the model's size — the crash killed the outline/label updates); `previewEventModel` stamps a `pickBox`.
- Per-event 3D refresh: `rr-events-changed` routes to `refreshEvents` (never the world rebuild); `_buildOneEvent` carved out of `buildEvents`, `_eventIdentity` snapshots drive the diff — effects, animated models, sheet animations (now tagged `eventId`), pickables/billboards/labels all follow their event out. `UIManager`'s capture-phase Delete yields to an active props selection via `remove()` (which records undo).
- Self-retrigger guard (runtime `20260830.25`): `Game_Event.start` no-ops while the map interpreter is running this same event (touch/action re-arm during background waits kept `_starting` true, vetoing the `isEventRunning` exemption). Runtime revision now also readable in the F12 console: `RPG_REACTOR_RUNTIME_REVISION`.
- Background waits unified (runtime `20260830.23`): the `Game_Map.isEventRunning` exemption covers `reactorModelAnimation` as well as `reactorScopedWait`.
- Scoped Wait's switch/variable fields read as themed dropdowns: the chosen name inline (`0007: Torch Lit`) with a caret, opening the picker on click.
- Scoped Wait polish: `SwitchVariablePicker` buttons (label `0007: Name`, z-bumped over the dialog), one 120px-label grid for every row, picker section `Game Flow` translated across the section tables and the reviewed sections catalog (de/ru blocks were empty one-liners).
- Scoped Wait resume toggle (runtime `20260830.22`): "This event keeps moving" checkbox (default on) gates the invoking event's unlock; unchecked, it stands locked the MZ way while the wait holds.
- Scoped Wait switch/variable modes (runtime `20260830.21`): decision extracted to `Reactor3D.scopedWaitHolding` (all four modes; actions keeps the 60 s deadline, switch/variable hold indefinitely); dialog gains Switch id + ON/OFF and Variable id + operator + value rows, radios auto-select on focus.
- Scoped Wait (runtime `20260830.20`): `ScopedWait` plugin command ({target, mode: actions|duration, duration}), `WaitForModelAnimation` registered as an alias; waitMode `reactorScopedWait` decided by duration or `Reactor3D.scopedActionsBusy` (animation queue + `isMoveRouteForcing`); `Game_Map.isEventRunning` reports false while the only running interpreter sits in a scoped wait (unless an event is starting), and the invoking event is unlocked at wait start. Dialog gains Last action / Duration radios.
- Wait for 3D (runtime `20260830.19`): plugin command `WaitForModelAnimation` ({target: id|0|-1|'all'}) holds via waitMode `reactorModelWait` on `Reactor3D.modelAnimationsBusy` (queue entries or playing holder actions; the all-scope also scans the scene's instances; 60 s deadline). `WaitForModel3DEditor` dialog, picker entry, command name + Everything in 17 locales.
- Animation queue (runtime `20260830.18`): `_modelActions[characterKey]` is a queue — `playModelAnimation` pushes (empty name clears), the 3D holder and flat-map consumers start the next entry when the stage is free, a repeating action stops repeating when something waits behind it, and `modelAnimationWaiting` scans the queue.
- Flash scopes + animation wait (runtime `20260830.17`): `flashTimings[].scope` (2 screen / 3 hide) written by the timing modal and honoured in `Sprite_Animation.processFlashTimings`/`updateFlash` (`_reactorHideDuration`, checkEnd waits for it); `PlayModelAnimation` takes `wait`, holds via waitMode `reactorModelAnimation` decided by `Reactor3D.modelAnimationWaiting` (pending → playing → released; repeat releases on restart; 30 s deadline). Tests: `animation-flash-and-wait.test.cjs`.
- Turn sweep (runtime `20260830.16`): `eventModelSweepHits` takes the from/to yaws and samples the real footprint along the short arc (whole-disc caution only when no arc is given); `eventModelCanFace` and `eventModelWouldOverlapEvents` skip characters that fail `charactersOverlapVertically`.
- `MapEditor3D.teardown` recomputes the 2D renderer size (`applyViewportCrop` + `renderMap({preserveScroll})`, one frame deferred) instead of trusting the size saved at enable time; `updateMapZoom` only ever updated the label.
- Prop events write `_direction` directly at setup (runtime `20260830.15`): the synthetic page carries direction-fix, under which `setDirection` is a silent no-op — the stored facing now wins unconditionally.
- Event list 505/code-45 rendering goes through `SetMovementRouteEditor.parseRouteCommand` (step names, translated) with the script text as fallback; `_buttonDefs` places rise/descend/height in column 1, the three poses in column 2, Rotate… beside Script in column 3.
- Placement trigger override (runtime `20260830.14`): `Reactor3D.rulesForPlacement(rules, name, repeat)` clones the chosen rule as `trigger: 'action'` (+ placement repeat); applied at the game holder's pending-fire and in `MapEditor3D.animateModel` drivers. DB editor: `_keysHtml` renders for every pose trigger and `_workValues` strips keys only from the live slider stand-in (`triggerOverride`); `_healAllAnchorBindings` runs after `applySelection` so a fresh carve claims origin-anchored effects; `deletePart` converts anchored offsets back to the model frame before unbinding.
- Whole-model target (runtime `20260830.13`): `prepareModelInstance` stamps the anim-root's rest turn and `effectAnchorNode('')` resolves to it, so `effectAnchorWorld`/`effectAnchorQuaternion` track whole-model poses for unanchored effects (offsets still read in model space on unprepared objects). `renderPartList` opens with a built-in Whole model row selecting `''`.
- Anchor lookup + heal (runtime `20260830.12`): `Reactor3D.effectAnchorNode` resolves a part name via node name OR `userData.parts` (overlap-carved pieces answer to every owner); `effectAnchorWorld`/`effectAnchorQuaternion` use it, as do the editor's place/convert/marker paths (`_anchorNode`). `Database3DEditor._healAnchorBinding` rebinds an `anchor.part: ''` effect to the smallest piece whose box contains its point, on `selectEffect`. Functional test drives a pose through `applyModelAnimation` in a three.js vm: `effect-anchor-tracking.test.cjs`.
- `_placeEffectAnchor` binds `anchor.part` from the hit (carved part name, or dominant bone on a rig; origin only when the click lands on unowned geometry) and stores the offset in that part's frame; `syncWork`'s part change converts the offset between the old and new frames and refreshes the live video preview.
- Part-anchored effects (runtime `20260830.11`): `prepareModelInstance` stamps `__restQuaternion` on posed nodes; new `Reactor3D.effectAnchorQuaternion` returns the anchor part's pose delta, applied in `reactor_video_surfaces` placement, `MapEditor3D.updateEffectPlays` and `Database3DEditor._updateVideoPreview`. Part rename also renames `rawEffects[].anchor.part` (+ open `_effectWork`) and redraws the effect list/form. `syncPropRings` orients rings from the placed object's real rotation instead of the spec's bare yaw/pitch.
- Database3DEditor effect flow: `selectEffect` auto-starts `_playVideoPreview` for video effects (and stops it for others); the render gate's `animating` includes `_fxVideo`/active `_fxPreview`; `_pickPart` returns early in effect card mode so the card survives model clicks (parts stay reachable via their list and the card chooser). `DatabaseEditorUI` nav + title fallback renamed to `3D Models`. Tests: `db3d-effects-flow.test.cjs`.
- Lights flat + poses (runtime `20260830.10`): `syncLights` writes quads on ground axes (right = +x, up = north; facade lift is plain vertical now); `reactorFacePose(±1|0)`/`reactorRotate(deg)` on `Game_CharacterBase`, applied to models after facing (`rotateX(-pose·π/2)`, `rotateZ(-spin)`) and to sprites as feet-anchored `rotation` in both flat and stood-up form. Route steps `faceceiling/faceground/standup/rotate` share the code-45 codec rails; `_dlgRotate` dialog; five new phrases ×17 locales.
- Rave light shim (runtime `20260830.9`): `raveYaw` negates both the smooth flashlight angle and `facingYaw` (clockwise-from-south → the scene's anticlockwise aim, matching the nova shim's negation). New `lightSolidGrid` (per-map cache; on room maps a cell with no tile is wall at room height) + `lightBlockHeightAt`/`lightSegmentBlocked`/`clampConeReach`; `syncLights` skips a light whose cell the camera cannot see and shortens cones to `beamReach`; all behind `Reactor3D.LIGHT_OCCLUSION`. Tests: `light-occlusion.test.cjs`.
- Prop axis arrows: `RRAxisArrows3D` on the selected prop (created/disposed with the pose rings in `selectPropRings`, synced in `syncPropRings`), grabbed before rings on pointer-down; `dragPropAlongAxis` slides x/y fractionally and clamps z to `PROP_MAX_LIFT`, deferring the sidecar rerender to `finishPropDrag` like the rings. Card tab renamed Coordinates (`_tx`, 17 locales).
- `ModelGraphicPicker._placeFaceAt` snaps a face mark's horizontal direction to the nearest 45-degree step within a 15-degree tolerance (the mark is what `eventModelWorldYaw` turns by, so a slightly-off dot was a permanent tilt); its pointer-move work (ring pick, pose apply) is coalesced to one `requestAnimationFrame` per frame. `MapEditor3D.refreshEvents` rebuilds just the event group (effects stopped via `disposeEffectPlays`, materials disposed, `buildEvents` re-run — which draws start markers itself) and `EventManager.renderEvents` calls it when the 3D view is up, so a changed event model shows at once. Tests: `event-editing-3d.test.cjs`.
- **Vertical coordinate (runtime revision `20260830.8`).** `_reactorLift` is every character's height (was props-only), eased to `_reactorZTarget` in `updateMove` at `distancePerFrame` (`isMoving` includes it, so Wait for Completion holds); `reactorRise/reactorSetHeight` clamp to the room's ceiling (`verticalCeiling`, default 64); events read `reactor3d.eventZ[id]` in `setupEvents`; `charactersOverlapVertically` (heights from `characterHeightTiles`) filters `isCollidedWithEvents` and `isCollidedWithPlayerCharacters`; `standingPlaceFor` lifts billboards; flat maps lift only props. Editor: `EventEditor` position inputs write x/y and `setEventZ` (z counts as a model change for the commit); `MapEditor3D.placeEvent/previewEventModel` add `eventZAt`; `RRAxisArrows3D` (new util) gives the selected event X/Z tile-stepped and Y free drags; `syncGridLevel` draws the level plane; both passage overlays skip placements with z > 0.5. Route steps: `SetMovementRouteEditor.routeCommand/parseRouteCommand` (codec kind `route`); tests `vertical-position.test.cjs`.
- **Ghost at full rate.** `updateHover` keeps `propAt` at ≤30 Hz (cached `_hoverOverProp`) and places the ghost on every move (one `groundPointAt` raycast); `_placementSpec` is memoized on a field signature.
- **Ghost flicker (owner-reported).** `ModelPropsManager._pointerMove` returns before the hover-ghost logic when `mapEditor3D.isEnabled()`; `_hideGhost(also3D)` hides the 3D ghost only from deactivate; the 3D canvas's `pointerleave` hides its own.
- **3D-M transform card + per-axis stretch (runtime revision `20260830.7`).** `ModelPropsManager._renderCard/_cardApply/_syncCard`: range+number rows per tab (offset x/y ±4 tiles around the prop, z 0–8; rotate ±180°; size 0.1–20 with `stretch` sliders when not proportional); a drag pushes one undo and updates silently, `_syncCard` moves numbers without rebuilding. `stretch: [x,y,z]` normalized in `MapElevation.normalizeProp` (omitted when all 1), carried by `specOf`/`propModelSpec`/`_normalizeModelSpecNow`, applied in `RREventPreviewModels.instance`, `poseProp`, the play-time instance, the collision mask (per-axis fit, pose keys) and the footprints.
- **Undo for props on the map history.** `MapEditor.recordPropsState` pushes `{ kind: 'props', mapId, data }`; `undo/redo` restore through `ModelPropsManager.snapshotProps/restoreProps`; the tab's own Ctrl+Z defers to the toolbar handler so a step is not taken twice.
- **Placement ghosts.** `MapEditor3D.updatePlacementGhost/hidePlacementGhost` (faded material clones of the instance, keyed by spec + facing, moved with `placeProp`); `ModelPropsManager._showGhost/_hideGhost` (the thumbnail sprite at half alpha under the cursor).
- **`TransformModel3DEditor` + runtime `Reactor3D.transformModel/liveTransformAt/applyLiveTransform`.** Plugin command `RPGReactor`/`TransformModel3D` (args target, offsetX/Y/Z, yaw/pitch/roll, proportional, scaleX/Y/Z, duration, wait); state on `character._reactorTransform` (from/to/frame/duration, eased in-out, a new command starts from the eased state); applied after the pose in the model loop; `wait` uses `Game_Interpreter.wait`. Props are addressed as `PROP_EVENT_BASE + id`. Names in 17 locales (`RR_EVENT_COMMAND_NAMES` and the reviewed commands catalogue), phrase "Prop" added. Tests `transform-model-3d.test.cjs`.
- **`SelectThemingShim` labels follow programmatic `value`/`selectedIndex` (owner: 3D-M Facing showed Down).** Each wrapped `<select>` gets own accessors over the native setters that call `refreshLabel`; the MutationObserver still covers re-populated options. All 75 selects benefit; no per-panel change.
- **Event-mode exit re-activates `ModelPropsManager` when `tilesetPaletteViewer.currentLayer === 'M'`** (`main.js` toolbar toggle), the counterpart of the deactivate on entry.
- **`eventModelContains` for stationary off-grid characters (runtime revision `20260830.5`).** `contains(_x, _y) || contains(_realX, _realY)` is for a gliding event (the trailing tiles of a vehicle); a prop placed at (16.56, 25.2) with `isMoving()` pinned false was also tested from (17, 25), adding column 18 to a 16–17 footprint. Now: off the grid and not moving → the real position only. Diagnosed with `scratchpad/fx-prop2.mjs` (game `eventModelOccupies` tiles vs `blockedTilesFor` for one prop, same spec and yaw).
- **`buildModelCollisionMask` rewritten (owner: "only the outline, not every triangle").** A `Uint8Array` grid over the reach replaces the `Set` of cell keys; a mesh past `DENSE_TRIANGLES` (50K) is read from `DENSE_SAMPLES` (250K) evenly strided vertices with no triangle work; below that, a triangle inside one cell marks that cell and only larger ones walk edges and fill. Same mask API (`has`, `touches`, `blocksTile`, bounds; `cells.size` → `count`). Benchmark `scratchpad/bench-mask.cjs`: 3,056,400 triangles, 259 ms → 4 ms, identical bounds.
- **Passage overlay, part two.** `TilemapManager.modelPlacements(mapData)` lists props and model-bound events (the previewed page, `MapEditor3D.previewPageIndex`) for both overlays; `MapEditor3D.drawPassage/setPassageVisible/refreshPassage` draw `LineSegments` and footprint quads on the ground at cell elevation (redrawn on `rebuild` and in-place prop edits); `ProjectController.refreshTilesetSurfaces` re-points `tilemapManager.currentTileset` at the saved tileset and refreshes both views. `'Model size (tiles)'` → `'Model size'` in the picker, the event page and all locales.
- **Passage overlay (`TilemapManager.drawPassage`, `map-passage` toggle).** `TilemapManager.tilePassage(data, w, h, flags, x, y, roomFloor)` is the pure rule (top solid non-star tile decides; empty = 0x0f unless a room floor); marks are a `PIXI.Graphics` above the layers, model footprints filled from `Reactor3D.blockedTilesFor` per prop as templates arrive (`_drawModelPassage`, generation-guarded); `updateTiles` and `ModelPropsManager._changed` call `refreshPassage` (one redraw per frame). Test `passage-overlay.test.cjs`.
- **Room floor passability (runtime revision `20260830.4`).** `Reactor3D.installRoomFloorPassage` (from `installPropHooks`) wraps `Game_Map.checkPassage`: on a 3D map with `reactor3d.room`, a cell with no solid non-star tile passes. Diagnosed with `scratchpad/fx-canpass.mjs`, which maps `#` model / `m` map / `e` event blocking around each prop: the reactor's "buffer" was `m` on all-zero cells.
- **Props tool vs Event tool.** `main.js` drops `modelPropsManager` on entering event mode and leaves event mode when the 3D-M tab is picked. **Mask build**: `buildModelCollisionMask` keys cells by number (`keyOf`), `blockedTilesFor` caches per template and pose (`template.userData.__reactorMasks`).
- **Collision by tile coverage (runtime revision `20260830.3`).** `mask.blocksTile(x, z)`: the tile centred at (x, z) is blocked when any of the four quarter-cells around its centre is occupied or at least half its sixteen are; `eventModelContains` uses it (a box: `|local| <= raw`, half covered counts) instead of `touches` with `COLLISION_BODY_RADIUS` at tile centres, which padded every model by 0.34 tile. `buildModelCollisionMask` marks corners and edges a hair inside the triangle and gives a zero-area (vertical) face no interior scan: a box's wall at x = 1.5 marked the cell beyond it, so a 3×1 bench came out 3.25×1.25. Measured on the Demo: consoles 3×3 → 3×2 tiles. `blockedTilesFor(template, json, spec, direction, x, y)` lists a placed model's tiles by the same rule; `MapEditor3D.showPropFootprint` (with `selectPropRings`/`syncPropRings`) and `ModelPropsManager._drawFootprint` draw them. The panel keeps one Size (`scale` written as 1, an old scale shown folded in). `AnimationPreviewLayer` reads its pixels only until `visibleFrames` is learned (two plays); `EffekseerScene.shouldMeasure` drops to every 30 frames once `MEASURE_HISTORY` looks are in.
- **`Database3DEditor.saveRules` clears `RREventPreviewModels` and calls `refreshMap3DView` (owner report: on-demand trigger still Always on the map until restart).** `templateFor` caches the template with `userData.reactorSidecar`. `ModelPropsManager.chooseModel` keeps `fields.animation/effect` when the same model is picked again (the owner's consoles 3 and 4 came out with Effect none this way); `modelEffectNames` lists effects with their trigger.
- **`MapEditor3D.refreshProps(ids)` re-poses in place (owner: "transform instead of destroy and recreate").** `poseProp` applies size/scale (the same fit as `RREventPreviewModels.instance`), `applyEventModelPose` for facing and turn, and `placeProp` for lift and position to the existing instance; `propIdentity` (model file/texture, animation, repeat, effect) is stamped at build and a mismatch, or a prop that is new or gone, falls back to `buildProps`. Effect plays and animation drivers hold the same object, so they ride along; the per-step WebGL context churn is gone with the rebuild.
- **`AnimationPreviewLayer.dispose()` loses its WebGL context (owner report: white screen after editing a prop's Size/Scale).** `ModelPropsManager` calls `refreshProps` on every live edit → `disposeProps` → `disposeEffectPlays` → `layer.dispose()`, which released the Effekseer context and removed the canvas but left the WebGL context to the collector; Chromium's budget is 16 live contexts, and eviction took `MapEditor3D`'s (`THREE.WebGLRenderer: Context Lost` → `fail`: "WebGL 2 is unavailable"). Every other context owner in the editor already calls `WEBGL_lose_context.loseContext()` on dispose; the layer now does too, and drops `_drawFrame`.
- **In-scene Effekseer effects at full resolution (runtime revision `20260830.1`).** `Reactor3D.EffekseerScene` no longer draws every effect at `SCALE` 0.5 of the screen: `trackedRect` projects a cylinder around the anchor (`frameBox`) to a screen box, drawn 1:1 with the full-screen viewport and a scissor, copied out and mapped back by the quad's `rectMin`/`rectSize` uniforms (default whole-screen, so existing quads are unchanged). A box past `BUDGET` (0.25 of the screen, the old cost) is drawn at `sqrt(budget/area)`. The reach is learned: `measure` downscales the drawn box to 64 px every `MEASURE_EVERY` frames and turns the lit extent into a radius and a below/above range in tiles (`upY`, the camera up axis's world y, corrects a steep camera's foreshortening); the widest of the last `MEASURE_HISTORY` looks is kept, a picture touching the box's edge grows that side by half, never past the authored `effectRadius` (1.25 frames). The tracker lives on the holder per effect name, so an Always effect's restarts keep it. Screen-fraction tracking was tried first and drifted as the camera turned; a sphere wasted pixels on a tall beam. Measured on the Demo core in third person: 640×360 stretched → ~350×300 at 1:1, `render` 0.6–0.75 ms/frame, no clipped frames static or orbiting. The map editor's 3D view (`MapEditor3D._placeEffectQuad`) keeps its mechanism (the layer draws on its own loop, the quad takes the latest picture each render, whole-view mapping) and only grows the layer's canvas from a 512 square to the view's size (≤ `EFFECT_PIXELS`) through `AnimationPreviewLayer.setWorld({ rect, viewWidth, viewHeight })`; `previewActive` now counts a playing effect, since with video previews off the view idled at 10 renders/s and the effect refreshed only on a render (choppy). A synchronous `drawNow` variant and a per-effect box were tried in the editor and dropped. The frozen, screen-bound effect the owner saw was the **resize**: three allocates a canvas texture immutably at its first size (`texStorage2D` on WebGL 2), so once the window grew, uploads of the bigger layer canvas failed and the quad kept its last frame stretched over the screen (a 512 square never resized, so the old code never hit it). Both the editor and the game (`scratch` changes size with the box) now `texture.dispose()` when the source changes size so the next upload reallocates (runtime revision `20260830.2`).
- **Seamless loops (`visibleFrames`).** The runtime learns where each play's picture ended (`play.lastLit`, from the same measurement; `track.visibleFrames` keeps the longest seen since a play the camera looked away from ends early) and `updateAnchoredAnimations` starts a loop's next play at that frame with `entry.restarted`, the tail running underneath. `AnimationPreviewLayer` and the Animations page preview do the same from a 32-px alpha readback of their own canvas; the page's `setTimeout(play, 100)` is gone. Harness: `scratchpad/fx-verify.mjs` (look up first: the default third-person camera cannot see the core), `fx-editor.mjs`.
- **Autotile pick keeps the piece (user report, `scratchpad/Autotile/`).** `selectPickedAutotile` sets `tileId` on the palette position; `exactAutotileId` stamps it for Shift-paint in the pencil, rectangle and preview when it is of the selected kind. Test in `autotile-edge-and-pick.test.cjs`.
- **Conditional Branch in RPG Maker's four-tab layout (user reports).** `ConditionalBranchEditor` renders `TABS` (1: Switch/Variable/Self Switch/Timer, 2: Actor, 3: Enemy/Character/Vehicle, 4: Gold/Item/Weapon/Armor/Button/Script, Reactor: keyboard/mouse/wheel/pointer) as `.rr-cb-row` radio rows; `_conditionRow` disables every control of an unchosen condition, `_choiceRow` handles the indented Constant/Variable and actor/enemy sub-choices, `_checkRow` the Include Equipment boxes. Switch/variable fields reuse `.rr-variable-reference` (the `0001 Name ...` button → `SwitchVariablePicker`); database fields are `0001 Name` selects. Timer edits minutes and seconds into the single `timerSeconds`. `show()` takes `options.troop` (both `DatabaseTroopEditor` call sites pass `currentTroop`) so the Enemy dropdown reads `#1 Gnome`. Parse/build/advanced-input serialization is untouched; the 72 branch tests and a CDP drive of every tab in dark and light pass. Nine new phrases hand-written in all 17 locales.
- **`ColorFilter.uniforms` on PIXI 8 (GitHub #28, runtime revision `20260829.39`).** The v8 `ColorFilter` (a `ColorMatrixFilter`) defines `_defineLegacyUniforms`: a `uniforms` object whose `hue`/`brightness` accessors call the setters and whose `colorTone`/`blendColor` are Proxies over the backing arrays, rebuilding the matrix on element writes; `setColorTone`/`setBlendColor` copy in place (`_copyInto`) so the views stay bound. v5–7 untouched. Contributed patch, reviewed; `colorfilter-uniforms-v8.test.cjs`.
- **One depth buffer on model maps (runtime revision `20260829.45`).** `MapEditor3D.render` used three passes with `clearDepth()` between them (below / star tiles / layer ≥ 5 video surfaces), so anything in a later pass painted over the world regardless of position; on a map with models (`Reactor3D._hasEventModelsNow`, which now counts `reactor3d.props` too) it renders the single `world` pass instead, and the runtime's `setPass("world")` takes `_aboveBillboardsGroup` along when `scene.modelsInWorld` is set by `updateReactor3D`. The editor's Effekseer effect previews on the map are no longer a DOM overlay: `AnimationPreviewLayer.setWorld` draws from the view's camera at the world anchor (512-px canvas) and `MapEditor3D._placeEffectQuad` shows it on `Reactor3D.EffekseerScene.quadFor` (`flip` uniform on: a WebGL canvas reaches three top-down whatever `flipY` says). The effect plane is no longer a constant screen-depth quad but a 600-tile vertical plane stood on the anchor and turned to the camera (`standQuad`), so a tall beam's depth follows its height — from above, a tower's base is farther along the view axis than the mid-height anchor, and the flat plane drew the beam over it.
- **Anchored Effekseer effects in the 3D scene (runtime revision `20260829.44`).** `Reactor3D.EffekseerScene`: Effekseer cannot draw on three.js's WebGL 2 context (its programs are WebGL 1; the attempt blacked out the frame) and a second Effekseer context fights the first over the library's one global object table ("object does not belong to this context" every frame, massive lag), so the effect stays on the overlay context: during the update its hidden sprite's own handle is drawn from the scene camera (`setProjectionMatrix`/`setCameraMatrix` from the three camera, `setLocation` at `effectAnchorWorld`, one unit = span × scale / 26 tiles) into a scissored corner of the overlay canvas at `SCALE` (0.5), the corner is copied through a 2D scratch canvas into a screen-sized `ShaderMaterial` quad whose `gl_Position.z` is the anchor's clip depth (world pass only, `setPass`), and `Sprite_Animation.renderActive` clears the overlay as it always did. `Sprite_Animation.updateEffectGeometry` skips a `_reactorInScene` sprite so the anchor owns the handle. `Spriteset_Map.updateReactor3D` calls `render(viewport)` before the passes. MV sheet animations stay on the overlay with the facing rule.
- **Show Text multi-box + text codes (GitHub #29; contributed, reviewed).** New `utils/MessageBoxes.js` (`collectRun`/`buildCommands`/`splitLines`; boxes keep `source` and an `untouched` box writes back byte-for-byte), `utils/TextCodes.js` (catalogue: vanilla, MessageCore families gated on `status: true` in the plugin manifest, project `TextCodeActions`/`TextCodeReplace`), `utils/TextCodeMenu.js` (`attach`, `createReferencePanel`, `openPluginHelp`), `utils/Windowskin.js` (`\C[n]` sampled from Window.png through `RREncryptedAssets.readAssetBytesAsync`, so encrypted projects get real colours), `utils/ColorPickerModal.js`; `MessageCommandEditor` holds `boxes[]`/`activeIndex`; `EventCommandList`, `DatabaseCommonEventEditor`, `DatabaseTroopEditor` share the run reader. Review changes over the contribution: the duplicate `IconPickerModal.js` was dropped for `RRIconPicker.show(…, { zIndex })`, scripts wired in `index.html`, the bare `require('path')` guarded for the web build, and the re-split policy above. 24 strings hand-written in 17 locales.
- **Database text codes (GitHub #30, runtime revision `20260829.43`).** `TextCodes.forScope` scopes `help` and `battlelog` (wrap and pictures reach help only; alignment both; the message-only waits, `\CommonEvent`, `\Wait` neither) plus a `FORMAT_ARGS` placeholder tier (`skillMessage` %1/%2, `stateMessage` %1); `<linebreak>`/`<NoWordWrap>` are the spellings MessageCore matches. `database/DatabaseTextCodes.js` decorates `[data-rr-textcodes]` fields from `DatabaseEditorUI.showDatabaseDetail` (menu, shared collapsed panel per spec, a plain-Event→`change` bridge so an inserted code persists). Runtime (contributed addendum): `Window_BattleLog.setOutcomeItem`/`displaySkillOutcome` show skill `message3` on a hit and `message4` on a miss, named only on `BattleManager.invokeNormalAction`. Tests: `message-text-codes`, `database-text-codes`, `skill-outcome-messages`.
- **Grow: Max TP + random range; Change Parameter: Max TP + Random (GitHub #15, runtime revision `20260829.42`).** `Game_BattlerBase.PARAM_MAX_TP = 8` routes `addParam` into `_maxTpPlus` (read by `maxTp`, `|| 0` for old saves; `Game_Enemy.maxTp` adds it to an authored value); `Game_Action.effectGrowValue` draws between `value1` and `value2` when `value2` ≠ 0; `operateValue(op, type, operand, operandMax)` type 2 = Random, `command317` passes `params[6]`. Editor: Special tab param list has nine entries (Buff stays eight), `_growRangeRowHTML`/`_readGrowRange` keep the non-zero end in `value2`, `getEffectValue` fixes the `+-5` sign and shows `+3–8`; Change Parameter dialog Random radio with two ends (seventh parameter written only then); command list shows `{V}` for variables and `a–b` for ranges. `effect-grow-max-tp.test.cjs`.
- **Add State duration override (GitHub #16, runtime revision `20260829.41`).** Effect code 21 reads `value2` as turns (0 = the state's own, which is what MZ writes) and an optional `value3` as the range's upper end (written only when it differs); `Game_Action.effectStateTurns` rolls it, `applyEffectStateTurns` calls the new `Game_BattlerBase.setStateTurns` after `addState` for both normal and attack states. Editor: `_durationRowHTML` is a sibling of the Add State row (the i18n pass and radio lookups read `.effect-option > span`), boxes default to the chosen state's Min/Max Turns and grey until ticked, Auto-Remove None disables it with a note ("Not removed automatically", 17 locales); `durationSummary` appends ", 3–6 turns". `effect-add-state-duration.test.cjs`.
- **Enemy Max TP + Parameters/Drop layout (GitHub #31, runtime revision `20260829.40`).** `Game_Enemy.prototype.maxTp` reads `enemy.maxTp` (number or numeric string; anything else falls back to `Game_BattlerBase.prototype.maxTp` looked up at call time, so plugin overrides keep governing unset enemies). Editor: `parameterRows` (Max TP under Max MP, `field: 'maxTp'`, no param index — never a ninth `params` entry), `paramInputWidth` (`calc(Nch + 48px)`, 2–9 digits), two-row drop slots with `formatDropRate` readout (`data-rr-i18n-skip`, since `applyText` pins `.database-field-label` text), reveal with `display: 'flex'`, top-row flex 0.9/1.6/0.9. `enemy-max-tp.test.cjs` (12 tests, contributed).
- **Referenced by (GitHub #23).** Contributed and reviewed: `database/DatabaseReferenceFinder.js` scans the live database's structured fields (trait codes 13/14/32/35/43/44, effect codes 21/22/43/44, drops, troop pages, 30 event command codes with the 111 subtypes and 605 shop rows; variable-targeted commands skipped) and `DatabaseEditorUI.showReferencesModal` renders the groups with rows that `reveal()` the record in its list. Two phrases in 17 locales; `database-referenced-by.test.cjs` (18 tests).
- **Plugin command `showFilePicker` is folder-aware (GitHub #7).** `PluginCommandEditor.extensionsForDir(dir)` picks the extension list from the `@dir` root (`img`, `audio`, `movies`, `fonts`, `effects`; anything else lists all kinds).
- **User Lacks State / Target Lacks State conditions (GitHub #32, runtime revision `20260829.39`).** `meetsActionCondition` types 9 and 10 negate `meetsStateCondition` / `meetsTargetStateCondition` (so a plugin replacing either moves both polarities); catalog rows beside their positive twins, summaries, 17-locale reviewed strings; five tests in `enemy-action-conditions.test.cjs`.
- **Play Model Animation lists the target's actions.** The animation field is a dropdown of the action rules the chosen target's model declares (`PlayModelAnimationEditor.modelActionNames` reads `3d/<name>/model.json`; an event's model comes from the map sidecar or its `<r3d>` note, the player's from the starting party leader's actor binding in `Database.r3d.json`), refilled when the target changes. A target with no model, or a model without actions, lists every action in the project with a note saying so; a saved name the model no longer declares stays selectable and marked; Custom… keeps a free-text path.
- **Preview Event in the 3D view.** Choosing a preview page while the 3D view is on now rebuilds the 3D event layer, so model-bound previews (vehicles, monsters) appear at once instead of at the next project open; `setEventPreview` refuses an event without an id rather than writing an `undefined` key into the sidecar.
- **The 3D view is remembered per map.** `ProjectController.map3DViewRemembered/rememberMap3DView` keep a per-project `localStorage` record (`rrMap3DViewMaps:<project>`) of which maps were left in 3D; `refreshMap3DView` reconciles the viewport with the loaded map's memory on every load (a 3D map comes back in 3D after reopening, switching to a 2D map switches the view), the toolbar checkbox records the choice, and a crash last session keeps remembered maps in 2D until the box is ticked by hand.

### Added
- **Live message miniature.** The Show Text editor draws the box being edited right under the text field — windowskin, face, name box, colours and icons resolved, using the project's own font and resolution. It follows the caret: with more lines than one box holds, the box under the cursor is the one shown, updating as you type (`renderMiniPreview`, guarded in `message-text-codes.test.cjs`).
- **GLB import optimizer.** `RRGlbOptimizer` (`src/utils/GlbOptimizer.js`) + `UIManager.showModelOptimizeDialog`: importing a GLB in the Resource Manager offers Optimize (2K textures, uint16 skin weights, tangent drop, duplicate-vertex weld — in-place accessor substitution keeps animations/skins intact by construction; the tangent pass GC-remaps every reference), aggressive (adds mesh simplification via position-bucket clustering with UV tolerance and a normal-dot guard), or as-is; Cancel aborts. `validateModelBytes` still gates the output; guarded by `glb-optimizer.test.cjs`.
- **Effect scale is model-relative (runtime revision `20260829.38`).** Scale 1 = the animation's authored screen (`Graphics.height` px; 26 Effekseer units, or MV cell pixels) is the model's longest side: `Reactor3D.effectModelScale(object)` = `modelSpanTiles(object)` × tileHeight / screenHeight multiplies the axes of an anchored animation (MV sheets carry it through the stand-in's `_reactorExtra`). `AnimationPreviewLayer.setSpan(tiles)` projects Effekseer with `q = span / 8` and MV cells at `(size / 8) × span / screenHeight`; the DB passes the model as shown (1.6 tiles), the map editor the prop instance. Replaces `effectInstanceScale`. Demo Core effect 0.22 → 2.05 (same look).
- **Effect card sliders drive the running preview; overlay at display size; F4 is not Escape (runtime revision `20260829.37`).** `_updateTriggeredEffectPreview` re-points `_fxPreviewDef` at the working copy when the playing effect is selected (it kept the saved record, so `_updateEffectPreview` re-applied the saved offset/scale every frame and the sliders looked dead). `AnimationPreviewLayer` grows its canvases with the overlay (`size`, 128-px steps, `MAX_SIZE` 1024) so a large effect is no longer a 384-px thumbnail stretched over the model. The camera's keydown listener marks F3/F4 (and `fullscreenchange`) with `look.suppressMenuUntil`; `lockReleased` ignores a lock drop inside that window.
- **Context submenus stay on screen.** A submenu (Set Starting Position, Player Facing, Preview Event, Quick Event) opened near the bottom or right edge ran off the window and showed only its first entry; `EventManager.keepSubmenuOnScreen` shifts it up by its overhang (never above the top) and opens it to the left when it would overhang the right.
- **Map context menu fixed.** The Player Facing submenu called an undeclared `tt()` inside `showContextMenu`, so every right-click on the map or an event (2D and 3D) threw before the menu appeared; it now translates through `I18n.tText` and `player-start.test.cjs` guards the scope.
- **Player start facing + 3D start markers (runtime revision `20260829.36`).** `System.json.startDirection` (2/4/6/8, absent = 2; MZ ignores it) read by `Game_Player.setupForNewGame`; `EventManager.setPlayerStartDirection` saves it from the map menu's Player Facing submenu (`eventCtx.playerFacing`, 18 locales), `createStartingPositionMarker` takes a direction and draws `EventManager.arrowPoints`; `MapEditor3D.buildStartMarkers` (called from `buildEvents`, refreshed by `refreshStartMarkers` from `renderStartingPositions`) draws box + `textLabel` (+ `startArrow` for the player) for player/boat/ship/airship, not pickable.
- **Effect preview to scale, instance scaling, facing occlusion (runtime revision `20260829.35`).** `AnimationPreviewLayer` projects Effekseer with `q = screenHeight / 384` (the game's unit = screen height / 26 rule; `setScreenHeight`, default from the project's System), draws MV cells at 1:1 (was 0.85) and lets the overlay grow to 8192 px (the 1024 cap shrank effects on big models). Runtime `effectInstanceScale(object)` = object scale × span / `DB_PREVIEW_SPAN` (1.6) multiplies an anchored animation's axes (`MapEditor3D` does the same for prop effect plays). `effectFacesCamera` hides an anchored sprite whose anchor sits within `EFFECT_FACE_DEPTH` (20%) of a side face (never the underside) when that face's normal points away from the camera, with ±0.05 hysteresis; `occlude: false` opts out. Demo's Core effect retuned 0.58 → 0.22 to keep its look under the corrected preview.
- **Look up over the shoulder, Escape calls the menu (runtime revision `20260829.35`).** A negative third-person pitch no longer orbits under the figure: `place` keeps the eye at the focus height behind the figure and pitches the view up, `resolve` slides the distance in by up to `LOOK_UP_CLOSE` (75%) toward `MIN_THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE`; the floor clamp is gone. `pointerlockchange` that loses the lock with the window focused is the browser-swallowed Escape: `lockReleased` sets `Scene_Map.menuCalling`.
- **Look up, unlocked mouse, head lean, floor clamp (runtime revision `20260829.34`).** `LOOK_PITCH.thirdPerson` is `[-25, 80]`; `mousemove` turns the look from client deltas when the pointer is not locked (`requestLook` asks for the lock on a key press or click); `Reactor3D.applyLookLean` pitches the head bone (else the body) by `Camera.lookLean()` (60% of the look past the default pitch, ±30°) after the animation pass, since a clip writes every bone each frame; `keepAboveFloor` keeps the third-person eye `FLOOR_CLEARANCE` (0.3) above the higher of the ground under the figure and under the eye by sliding in to `MIN_THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE` (1.5) and then levelling the pitch. The game context supplies `groundAt`.
- **Mouse look + camera-relative WASD (runtime revision `20260829.31`).** The camera module keeps a `look` (yaw/pitch, seeded from the facing, driven by pointer-lock `mousemove` at 0.15°/px, pitch clamped per mode) that `resolve` uses for the player-relative modes; `held` keys (WASD/arrows) map through `relativeMove()` to a map direction or diagonal pair relative to the camera yaw, and the `moveByInput` wrapper walks it (`moveDiagonally`, falling back to either axis); first person turns the body to the look when still. Touch destinations are cleared in these modes. Listener install is guarded for sandboxes without `document.addEventListener`.
- **Collision follows the model's geometry by default (runtime revision `20260829.30`).** `readModelCollision` defaults to `mesh` (`collision: "box"` opts a model out); `modelCollisionMask` rasterises triangles below walking height at `COLLISION_SUBDIV` = 4 cells per tile (vertices, sampled edges, cell-centre fill) and answers `mask.touches(x, z, radius)` for a walking body of `COLLISION_BODY_RADIUS` 0.34 tiles, which `eventModelContains` uses instead of whole-tile tests; footprints are built under the loading fade in `preloadMapModels` (a three-million-triangle model takes ~300 ms once). Demo reactor: 117 blocked tiles as a round base vs 173 for its box; ~1 µs per check.
- **Video effect size is a fraction of the model (runtime revision `20260829.29`).** `video.width/height` are fractions of the model's longest side (`videoEffectFraction`, with values above 4 read as pixels ÷ 96 so saved effects do not explode); `videoEffectSize(effect, glbSize)` gives the plane in model units for the runtime's `anchor.size` and both editor previews.
- **Video effects are sized in model units (runtime revision `20260829.28`).** `video.width/height` are the model's own units, like the anchor offset; the runtime sends `anchor.size` with the surface and `updateThreePosition` scales the plane to units × the model's world scale over its tile-sized geometry, and both editors parent the preview plane to the model at that size, so the screen matches across the database, the map editor and the game.
- **3D database sidebar: folding sections, click-off deselect, rename without replay.** Parts / Animations / Effects headers fold their bodies (`toggleSection`); a click on a list's empty space deselects (effect → `_leaveEffectMode`, rule/part → `deselectPart`); the state-triggered effect preview is keyed by list index, so typing a new name no longer restarts a playing video.
- **Anchored effects scale with the world (runtime revision `20260829.27`).** `placeStandIn` gives the stand-in a `reactor3DScale` of (screen pixels one tile covers at the anchor) / tile size, which `Sprite_Animation.reactor3DScale` averages exactly as it does for characters, so an effect shrinks into the distance with its model instead of playing at flat pixel size; the model's yaw joins the effect's rotation. Both editors' overlays use the same rule (384 native pixels span eight tiles) and add the model's yaw.
- **Video effects, mesh collision, relative controls, editor playback (runtime revision `20260829.26`).** Effects gain `type: 'video'` + `video: {file,width,height,loop,audio,volume}`; `spawnVideoEffect/stopVideoEffect` drive `RPGReactorVideoSurfaces.manager.show/stop` with an `anchor` the surface descriptor now carries (`normalizeShowArgs`), and `updateThreePosition` places an anchored surface at `effectAnchorWorld` turned with the model (`Reactor3D.modelHolderFor`). `readModelCollision`/`modelCollisionMask` (triangles below `COLLISION_WALK_HEIGHT` rasterised into cells in the body frame, cached per model+pose; `_sidecarJson` keeps model.json for the synchronous footprint) feed `eventModelFootprint.mask` and `eventModelContains`. The camera module wraps `Game_Player.moveByInput` for third/first person. `playModelAnimation(character, name, { repeat })`; props carry `repeat`. The holder loop now runs effects outside the rules gate (models without rules never fired them) and `updateAnchoredAnimations` restarts loops after its pass with `MAX_ANCHORED_PER_MODEL` (the in-pass restart recursed through `spawnAnchoredAnimation`). Editor: `RREventPreviewModels.animate` (carve/rig + binding + rules) and `MapEditor3D.animateModels/playModelEffects/updateEffectPlays` run rules, Always effects and a prop's chosen effect in the 3D view (animation overlays via `RRAnimationPreviewLayer`, video planes); `propAt` picks by `Box3` with hover throttled; `ModelPropsManager` undo/redo snapshots, Repeat checkbox, every rule listed; `Database3DEditor` effect Type/video fields, `_playVideoPreview`, Transform-card collision checkbox, `mergeSidecar(..., collision)`.
- **Props panel via the model picker (runtime revision `20260829.23`).** `ModelPropsManager.openModelPicker` shows `ModelGraphicPicker`; the result's size/yaw/pitch/roll become the prop's (`fields` carry the pose; the selected prop is updated in place). `_modelChoices/_fillChoiceSelects` list the chosen model's action rules and effects; props gain `animation`/`effect` (sidecar + `Reactor3D.normalizeProp`), queued by `installPropHooks` after `setupEvents` through `playModelAnimation`/`playModelEffect`. The inline model list is gone.
- **Effect triggers, per-axis scale, global steppers (runtime revision `20260829.22`).** `Reactor3D.readScale/scaleAxes` accept a number or `[x, y, z]` for `transform.scale` and effect `scale` (free scale patches the anchored sprite's `updateEffectGeometry` to call `setScale` per axis after the stock pass; the preview layer scales the same way). Effects gain `trigger`; `updateTriggeredEffects` spawns a looping anchored animation while the state holds and `stopAnchoredAnimation` ends it (entries carry `triggered`). Editor: `_fxPreviewDef` is the live working effect so anchor placement/drag and sliders move the preview at once; `_scaleSlidersHtml/_bindScaleSliders` (Proportional checkbox ↔ X/Y/Z) shared by the effect and transform cards; Play-when select on the effect card; `_updateTriggeredEffectPreview` runs state-triggered effects from the sim. `utils/NumberSteppers.js` wraps every `input[type=number]` (MutationObserver for later ones; `data-no-stepper` opts out) and theme.css hides native spinners.
- **3D editor card rework (runtime revision `20260829.21`).** `utils/SearchSelect.js` (grouped, searchable dropdown; `RRSearchSelect.create`) replaces the card's native part select (`_mountCardChooser`/`_onCardChooserPick`: `__model`, part/bone names, `fx:<name>`). Card modes: `_renderEffectCard` (Offset/Rotate/Scale sliders in the model's native units and degrees, Loop, Place toggle, Play, Save) and `_renderTransformCard` (Transform | Animations tabs; base transform sliders, Clear, Save transform) beside the existing pose card; the card sits at `top:10px`. `Reactor3D.readModelTransform/applyModelTransform` wrap every instance's children on a `base-transform` group (all five runtime instance sites, the editor viewport, event previews); `mergeSidecar(..., transform)` writes model.json `transform`. Effects gain `rotate` (degrees) applied with `scale` on a copy of the animation record in `spawnAnchoredAnimation` and live in `RRAnimationPreviewLayer.setTransform`. Rules gain `repeat` (runtime restarts the action on its `until`; an empty Play Model Animation name stops it; the editor preview loops repeat and non-on-demand rules). The effect anchor marker drags in the camera plane (`fxdrag`). Sidebar headers use `.sidebar-header`.
- **Model effects (runtime revision `20260829.20`).** `Reactor3D.readModelEffects` reads `effects` in model.json (`{ name, animation, anchor: { part, offset }, scale, loop, se, flash }`); rule timelines accept `{ at, effect }`; `fireModelEffect` resolves named effects; `playModelEffect/takeModelEffects` queue by character; `spawnAnchoredAnimation` plays a database animation through `Spriteset_Base.createAnimationSprite` aimed at a hidden stand-in sprite that `updateAnchoredAnimations` moves to `effectAnchorWorld` (the anchor part's or model's frame plus offset) projected through the map camera each frame, looping when asked; flat maps fall back to the character. `PlayModelEffect` plugin command. Editor: `Database3DEditor` Effects list/form (name, animation via the animation picker, anchor part/bone, offset, Place on model = `fxanchor` tool click → local offset in the anchor's frame, scale, loop, SE via the audio picker, Play, Save), a pink anchor marker, `utils/AnimationPreviewLayer.js` (transparent MV/Effekseer overlay moved to the projected anchor), named effects in rule timelines with preview, `mergeSidecar(..., effects)`. `PlayModelEffectEditor` (dropdown of the target model's effects) wired like Play Model Animation. Animation picker: black default backdrop, no scrollbar; the palette's props tab reads 3D-M.
- **Model props (runtime revision `20260829.19`).** `RRMapElevation.props/propById/addProp/updateProp/removeProp` keep `reactor3d.props` (`{ id, name, ext, file, texture, x, y, z, yaw, pitch, roll, direction, size, scale, passable }`, position in tiles, fractional allowed, angles in degrees, `size` the model's longest side in tiles). `Reactor3D.installProps` (called from `DataManager.loadMapSidecar`) stands each prop in `$dataMap.events` at `PROP_EVENT_BASE + id` (10000+) as a model-bound event (`reactor3d.events[id][0]` gets the model spec) so rendering, facing, pose and footprint collision are the existing event-model machinery; `installPropHooks` (installed from `reactor_sprites.js`) sets the free `_realX/_realY`, `_reactorLift` and pins `isMoving` false so the position does not slide home; model instances add the lift to their ground height and flat-map sprites rise by it. Editor: `ModelPropsManager` (palette M tab panel, PIXI sprite layer drawn with the Preview Event thumbnails, tile-snapped drag, Delete, selection sync), `MapEditor3D.buildProps/refreshProps/propAt/groundPointAt/selectProp/pickPropRing/dragPropRing/dragPropTo` (free placement, ground drag, pose rings), `utils/PoseRings3D.js` (the ring gizmo shared out of the video-surface code), `TilesetPaletteViewer.tabIcon('model3d')`. Sixteen `props.*` strings hand-written in all 18 locales.
- **3D camera modes (runtime revision `20260829.18`).** `Reactor3D.Camera` (in `reactor_3d.js`, also `RPGReactorCamera3D`) holds `MODES` (fixed/topDown/isometric/thirdPerson/firstPerson with pitch/yaw/fov/distance/focus/lift), `normalizeState`, `mapDefault` (`reactor3d.camera` gains `mode`; older `{pitch,yaw,distance,fov}` sidecars still read as fixed with overrides), `normalizeArgs`, pure `resolve(state, context)`, `step` (ease-in-out over frames, shortest-arc yaw, `TURN_RATE` follow for player-relative modes), `place` (first person positions the camera at the eye; other modes go through `aimCamera`), and `update(spriteset)`, which `Spriteset_Map.updateReactor3DCamera` delegates to. `Game_Map.setup` resets the state to the map default unless `$gameSystem._reactorCamera3d` keeps a commanded one; `Game_Map.reactorCamera3D()/setReactorCamera3D(state, duration, keep)`; interpreter wait mode `reactorCamera3D`; `Reactor3D.characterHiddenByCamera` hides the party's sprites, billboards, and models in first person. Hooks and the `ChangeCamera3D` plugin command are installed from `reactor_sprites.js`, after the classes they wrap exist (`reactor_3d.js` loads before them).
- **Map Properties chrome.** Field labels are 12.5px semibold (`.map-props-label`), every number field (Width, Height, Encounter Steps, Room Height, camera Pitch/Yaw/Distance/FOV) and the Change 3D Camera dialog's numbers wear the themed `rr-number-stepper` from the Video Surface editor (blank camera fields step from the mode's placeholder value), and Browse buttons share the accent `map-props-btn primary` style with Choose and Add.
- **Change 3D Camera editor + Map Properties Default Camera.** `event/commands/Camera3DEditor.js` (mode, pitch/yaw/distance/FOV with mode placeholders, focus + event, duration, wait, keep; `Camera3DEditor.build` is the serializer) is wired into the picker's 3D section, `EventCommandList._reactorCommandEditor`, and the Common Event editor's insert/edit paths. `RRMapElevation.camera/setCamera` store the map default (dropped when it is the stock view); `map-3d-camera-*` controls read/write it through `saveMap3DSettings`. Twenty-eight `cam3d.*`/`mapProps.defaultCamera` strings and the command name hand-written in all 18 locales, including the Polish reviewed catalog.
- **Map room (runtime revision `20260829.17`).** `Reactor3D.roomFor`, `roomImageNames`, `clampRoomHeight` read `reactor3d.room` (`{ height, floor, walls, ceiling }`, height 1-512 tiles like the map size, default 4). `MapScene.build` calls `addRoom` after the parallax grounds: floor a layer step under the parallax (tiled at image scale), ceiling at `height` facing down, four `FrontSide` walls facing inward whose image height is the wall height and repeats along it by aspect. Meshes are tagged `userData.roomPiece`, `renderOrder -2`, `__reactorShaded`. `MapScene.clear` bumps a `_build` token so a bitmap that finishes loading after a rebuild cannot lay a piece into the new scene. `RRMapElevation` gains `room`, `setRoom` (dropped when all defaults), `setMode3D`, and keeps a sidecar that holds only a room. `MapEditor3D.loadParallaxes` fetches the room images through the same loader.
- **Map Properties 3D section and audio picker flow.** `map-3d-checkbox` mirrors `RRMapElevation.hasNote` and is stripped from/added back to the note on save (a typed `<3d>` still counts); `saveMap3DSettings` writes the sidecar through `TilemapManager.saveMapSidecar` for the loaded map or `RRMapElevation.save` for any other (`editMap` loads the sidecar for a map read from disk) and refreshes the 3D view when the room or switch changed. BGM/BGS keep `{name, volume, pitch, pan}` in `_mapAudio`, rendered as a track row plus levels line, and `openMapAudioPicker` opens `RRAudioPickerModal` with the levels cards; `populateAudioDropdown`, the preview transport, and the inline steppers are removed. `fillParallaxSelect` serves the parallax background and the three room images. Fourteen `mapProps.*` strings hand-written in all 18 locales.
- **GitHub #6, Resource Manager.** `ResourceManager` provides a locale-sorted searchable recursive catalog for all MZ image/audio categories, `effects/`, `movies/`, `fonts/`, `icon/`, and Reactor `3d/`. It retains logical and physical identities for plain, encrypted, case-sensitive, nested, and same-name multi-format assets. Image/audio/video/font previews use the shared resolver; `ModelPreview3D` reuses lazy `MapEditor3D` library acquisition plus `Reactor3D.readModelAsync`, template cloning, camera aiming, sibling textures, orbit/zoom, idle throttling, pako-backed archives, generation cancellation, and dedicated-context disposal. Ctrl/Cmd and Shift batch export preserves nested paths and decrypts selected files. Ordinary desktop imports validate names/extensions/signatures, collision approval, symlink-free ancestry, project identity, and the live lock before `RRWriteFileAtomicSync`. The deletion-disabled 3D category separately allows one validated model import into a newly named nested folder: GLB/OBJ/FBX/STL/USDZ/3MF/DXF geometry is staged as `source/<case-preserved file>` with empty `textures/`; `.blend` is refused with export guidance; embedded GLB dependencies, triangle accessors, source/staging identities, ownership, an exact destination reservation, and a per-destination lock are checked before publish. It never merges/replaces an existing folder and rolls back owned staging/empty parents on write, commit, or concurrent-destination failure. Web remains browse/preview/export-only.
- **Native Video Surface commands and live authoring.** `ShowVideoSurface`, `TransformVideoSurface`, and `StopVideoSurface` remain code-357 `RPGReactor` commands, isolated from stock 261 and `PSYCHRONIC_VideoOverlay`. Runtime PIXI handles 2D/all screen targets, projectively warps corners, leaves Z to the 3D view (the dragged position is the whole 2D placement), and culls in screen pixels; rectangular Three.js planes handle 3D map/event/player targets with Z/world-camera culling and ignore corners. Both retain generations/waits, anchors, autoplay retry, same-map time, and deterministic cleanup. `VideoSurfaceEditor` authors layer/opacity, audio/playback rate, loop/wait, scanlines, culling, position/size/rotation/scale/depth/Z, and corners. Preview PIXI warps projectively, Three.js stays rectangular and applies culling, and 3D-screen DOM reshapes only a bounding-box clip; no editor preview applies scanlines. Every preview moves directly and shares anchors, field synchronization, lifecycle cancellation, and exact source navigation. Pages reduce independently without page-condition/runtime-flow evaluation; preview media is muted/looping. Live map previews start on a placeholder and swap to the movie once a frame has decoded (PIXI's load restart aborted the early `play()`, leaving a transparent panel); edge handles resize along their normal while corners warp; authoring pans the surface into the part of the viewport the side panel does not cover, and the panel drags by its title. Changing the target converts X/Y so the surface stays where it is (screen pixels, map tiles, or anchor offsets), Event sits beside Target and is editable only for Selected Event (otherwise greyed, naming this event, Player, or None), other fields show only what the target uses, and the live panel can pop out into its own window for a second monitor. Saved surfaces are clickable on the map: a click opens the panel on their Show command (OK writes it back, Go to Event jumps to it in the event editor), and the event command list names Reactor commands by what they do instead of Plugin Command. 2D previews now stack among the editor's tile layers the way the game sorts them by Layer (below every tile when negative, between lower and upper tiles up to 3, above upper tiles at 4, above characters from 5) and re-stack live as the field changes; the panel's numeric fields use the themed step buttons. Map, event, and player surfaces now stand on their anchor in 2D as they already did in 3D (runtime revision `20260829.5`), the 3D drag keeps the grabbed point under the cursor, editor previews draw the authored scanlines on every backend at the same 1px-every-other-pixel pitch as PSYCHRONIC_VideoOverlay (0.5 matches the plugin's strength), typed values snap to their range instead of failing validation, and a Video box beside A1/Grid/3D hides saved previews. Preview Event (event right-click) draws a chosen page's graphic at its real size and position under the marker, remembered per event in the map's Reactor sidecar. In the 3D view, surfaces on layer 5 and up now render in the game's above-characters overlay pass, composited over star tiles and world geometry alike, instead of being depth-tested inside the star-tile pass. Depth now also pushes a surface toward the camera in 3D (tiles, positive = closer) while the 2D feet row stays the reference; in 2D it remains the within-layer sort key. Preview Event now works in the 3D view too (the previewed page replaces page 1's billboard), plays stepping animation at the game's cadence in both views, and previews 3D-model pages as the placed model in 3D and a front-facing render at footprint size in 2D. 3D-model events now work on maps that are not rendered in 3D: the runtime draws them as sprites, a front orthographic render of the model at footprint size that turns with the character and plays its animation rules (runtime revision `20260829.6`), which is exactly what the 2D Preview Event shows. The map note is now the only 3D switch (a sidecar can downgrade a `<3d>` map to 2D but never promotes a map without the tag), and the sidecar loads for every map on disk so a flat map still gets its event models and previews (runtime revision `20260829.8`); sprite-mode models use the scene's exact pose and animation driver, and the 2D video backend no longer aborts its own load (the video played in 3D but failed silently in 2D). Model sprites on flat maps (and their editor previews) are now rendered from the map's 55-degree pitch, the angle the 3D view defaults to, framed about the model's ground origin so turning never shifts it, with that origin on the tile centre: a car facing right shows roof and side, facing down shows hood and windshield, instead of an eye-level cutout lying on the floor (runtime revision `20260829.9`). Flat-map model sprites paint on the standalone renderer and never acquire the shared 3D viewport (its PIXI state reset was breaking 2D lighting plugins after a scene change and tripping three's texImage3D setup); three now starts on PIXI's context with the unpack flags cleared; culled character sprites keep their position current so plugin sprites they steer (lights) no longer freeze off-screen; the v5 Graphics API (beginFill, drawRect, ...) is provided by silent compat shims instead of PIXI's nagging ones (runtime revision `20260829.16`). The Demo's PSYCHRONIC_RaveLighting no longer re-attaches beam sprites a scene change destroyed. PSYCHRONIC_MenuManagerMZ (Demo and Star Shift Freelancers) gates its layout/font diagnostics behind a new Debug Logging parameter, off by default. New event priority Above Characters (Sorted by Y): the page draws on the above layer like Above Characters, but a character standing in front of it and overlapping it is lifted onto that layer for the frame and y-sorted over it, while one behind stays under it (runtime revision `20260829.16`). It fixes the classic MZ clip where a tall party walks into a console that has to sit above the tiles. The 3D view is unchanged for now: there the page is an ordinary depth-tested billboard, the same as Above Characters. In the 3D view, the surface being authored now carries the model picker's pose rings (yaw green, pitch red, roll blue) around it; dragging a ring writes the matching rotation field, so a video is turned to its angle in place instead of by typing. Turning the 3D view off while a Preview Event model was on the map no longer aborts the scene teardown (which left the 3D box stuck and any open video panel with it). The sidebar's Tileset Palette, Events, and Maps headers carry the accent strip the Plugin Manager and event editor sections use. Runtime revision `20260828.3` adds `reactor_video_surfaces.js` to the boot manifest across all ten bundled projects.
- **PNG-compatible modern image pipeline.** `RRAssetFiles` keeps PNG extensionless while preserving explicit JPG/JPEG, WebP, safe SVG, and GIF for converted battleback, parallax, enemy-detail, title, animation, event character/face, Change Actor Images, Change Vehicle Image, System 1 vehicle, Picture, Resource Manager, Reactor UI Picture, and runtime paths. Explicit names retry legacy `<name>.<ext>.png`. Import validates signatures and rejects SVG scripts, handlers, external references, animation, styling, and URLs. Runtime preserves formats/encrypted MIME and refreshes animated GIF textures. Tilesets, plugin fields, database actor/list thumbnails, Reactor UI character/face/party-face/System/Title/Icon sources, balloon sheets, and fixed system sheets such as IconSet remain PNG-oriented.
- **Trusted native codec overlays.** Exact NW.js release matching, checked-in archive hashes, single-binary content validation, separate `.nw-codec-cache` storage, and clean official-runtime acquisition when disabled prevent stale overlays from contaminating output. Full desktop packages default to the overlay and carry `rpg-reactor-codec.json`, `RPG_REACTOR_CODEC_NOTICE.txt`, the complete LGPL 2.1 text, immutable `nwjs-ffmpeg-prebuilt`/FFmpeg source revisions, hashes, and patent guidance; Minimal and Web packages remain codec-free.
- **RPG Catalyst community forum links.** `https://rpgcatalyst.com` appears as an `external-link` in the HTML Help submenu, projectless welcome screen, and About link group; the NW.js native Help menu has the same entry through `nw.Shell.openExternal()`. The proper product name is registered across all localization dictionaries, and shell coverage verifies all three HTML placements plus native-menu browser dispatch.
- **GitHub #27, multiple enemy action conditions.** Enemy action patterns now author an AND list through a checklist for Turn, HP, MP, TP, User State, Target State, Party Level, and Switch. Named parameters replace Param 1/2, range fields use exact clamped percentages, state/switch rows preserve orphan IDs, and summaries show every condition with accurate turn recurrence. Target State evaluates every living/dead candidate reachable by stock scopes 0-14 before target selection; single-target skills are gated but retain normal target choice. Runtime dispatch and all eight helpers live on `Game_Battler` for actor auto-battle compatibility, malformed list records fail closed, and empty lists remain Always. Legacy triples remain the fallback and mirror the first saved entry; unsupported plugin types, extension fields, duplicate records, and Cancel survive editor interaction. Four phrases are reviewed in all 17 non-English locales. Runtime revision `20260828.2` refreshes the synchronized bundle.
- **Animation capacity and stable timing layout.** The workload-safe Animations ceiling rises from 1,000 to 5,000 throughout database growth and event-command ID entry. SE/Flash timings now occupy an independent right-side scrolling column so long timing lists no longer stretch or reshape animation Properties and preview controls.
- **One album-art cache for every editor audio surface.** `RRAudioCoverArt.forFile()` centralizes extraction and Promise caching for the main Audio Player, `RRAudioPickerModal`, System 1 music, all 24 system-sound slots, event-command audio, and plugin audio parameters. Picker headers and selected rows request art eagerly while unselected rows remain lazy; embedded images and deterministic placeholders retain their existing formats.
- **GitHub #12, system-sound variant pools and pitch ranges.** All 24 `System.json` sound slots now open a dedicated draft modal: the stock primary plus any number of uniformly selected variants, each authored through the shared recursive audio picker with its own volume/pitch/pan, and an optional absolute random pitch range clamped to 50-150. Add/remove/reorder and nested picker Cancel remain local until the outer OK; unknown slot and variant keys survive confirmation. System 1 exposes the seven formerly hidden slots Miss through Use Skill and summarizes pool size/range in its table. `SoundManager` resolves and preloads every valid candidate, copies only when applying random pitch, and preserves exact stock behavior without consuming RNG when no extension is configured. The inline `variants` and `pitchRandom` keys deliberately leave the stock primary fields intact, so older Reactor and stock MZ play that primary; RPG Maker MZ's own editor can drop the optional Reactor keys when it rewrites `System.json`.
- **GitHub #10, editor-only database names.** Actors, Classes, Skills, Items, Weapons, Armors, Enemies, and States gain an optional Editor Name injected by the shared database detail hook. The id-keyed `data/Database.names.json` sidecar stays out of RPG Maker records and game deployments while participating in Cancel, Apply, dirty-state, list Undo, pruning, duplicate, and version-3 cross-project database clipboard payloads. Search always includes both names; Options chooses Editor Name first, Game Name first, or Game Name only, and typed plugin database references use the same labels. The sidecar is dormant when absent, preserves unknown top-level keys, refuses to overwrite malformed content, and saves before normal database files so a rejected sidecar cannot leave them partially written. Database dialog saves now route through `ProjectController.saveProject()` so Web IndexedDB writes are flushed before success.
- **User Interfaces editor UX redesign.** `DatabaseUserInterfaceEditor` keeps the searchable ARIA Use As combobox and pointer-derived Custom semantics in a 44-56px toolbar on wider layouts, using a compact 70px two-row toolbar at 1280x720, with Name, Presentation, Interface Settings, and Playtest. Background, initial focus, cancel, overlay visibility, transitions, duration, and Note now render and update through concise Inspector groups. At 1280x720 the Inspector starts as a closed contained drawer whose current state is Interface Settings; selecting a layer or pressing Interface Settings opens it. Detail panes wider than 1050px keep all three panels in-row, while one-column reflow begins at 620px. The bounded workspace always fits height in both dimensions. Layers retain Back -> Front ordinals, subtree reorder/reparent, keyboard movement, and explicit Game Reference imports, with shorter visible order labels and full accessible names.
- **Typed Lists, named contexts, actor bindings, and display-only overlays.** `LIST_SOURCES` now covers `party`, categorized `inventory`, actor `skills`, `actorParameters`, `actorEquipment`, `actorStates`, `options`, `saveSlots`, `variableRange`, and `literal`. Rows carry source-qualified keys plus typed identity/display fields, publish immediately under an authored `contextName`, survive refresh by key, and can store ID/value before their action. Text, `partyFace`, Gauge, and actor Lists share `partySlot` / `actorId` / `menuActor` / game-variable actor ID / named-context binding. `{actor.*}` spans identity, level/profile, resources, EXP, and parameters; `{context.*}` spans typed row details. `Window_ReactorUIList` remains a real `Window_Selectable`. Overlay records attach only to `Scene_Map`, reevaluate visibility, and deliberately expose no focus or activation path.
- **Gauge expansion and complete generated baselines.** Gauge kinds now include HP/MP/TP, level-relative EXP, MHP/MMP, ATK/DEF/MAT/MDF/AGI/LUK, and variable; maximums may be authored or variable-backed, values format as current/current-max/percent/hidden, and label, two bar colors, back color, and bar height are authorable. `RRStockInterfaces.KINDS` appends stable IDs 4-7 without renumbering the first three: 1 Title Screen, 2 Main Menu, 3 Game End, 4 Status, 5 Options, 6 Save, 7 Load. Existing interface files are unchanged; tracked Demo records match generation and all seven System binding fields remain absent by default. This completes and supersedes the initial three-baseline stage recorded below.
- **Functional Options and asynchronous Save/Load adapters.** `options` rows expose MZ's booleans and four volume symbols; left/right clamps by 20, confirmation wraps/toggles, the row refreshes, and direct or replacement Options interfaces persist `ConfigManager` on termination. `saveSlots` rows expose slot title/playtime/date/party metadata plus existing/enabled state. `saveSlot` and `loadSlot` deactivate input and reject duplicate activation while the Promise is pending, preserve stock before-save/map-reload/after-load sequencing, reactivate with a buzzer on failure, and transition only after success.
- **Seven post-plugin-safe replacement roles.** `ReactorUI.REPLACEMENTS` is exactly Title, Main Menu, Status, Game End, Options, Save, and Load. Records carry explicit `roles`; zero, missing/malformed, overlay, ID-mismatched, and role-mismatched selections route stock. `installSceneRouting()` wraps the latest plugin-provided `SceneManager.goto/push` again at boot and immediately before `SceneManager.run`, with recursion-safe stock bypass. Main Menu's actor context opens stock Skill/Equip or the configured Status role. Item, Skill, Equip, Shop, Formation, Name Input/message inputs, and Battle have no replacement role until dedicated workflow adapters exist.
